c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-b75932c41633adf62fb4c0ea9ef63b0e][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-7b8d8e40efa651010f5a456f6a7d5367][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-815154c7fd721875c0bf79a2b79322d3][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-d6b0b5cb027d9b6200709d5d645c9d07][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^f00012][^f00013][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^f00014][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-1c7fa43536719df43679f302e45c47f2][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-0be82745d1f242d7e38ebde689cd85ff][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-c67600b22411c947839d251b9bd98ff0][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-41fad820e610fcd8946fd598c07d2a61][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-f564f1e34f625832c9a40af69beb28e9][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-f564f1e34f625832c9a40af69beb28e9][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852]

## ANNEX III (affected by repeal, see note) — Capped Portfolio Guarantee for SME's (Capped Guarantee) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852]

**Schematic representation of the Capped Guarantee *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852]**

Relation between stakeholders and Capped Guarantee portfolio coverage *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852]

[image: see source](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/eur/2014/964/images/eur_20140964_2019-02-16_en_002)[image: see source](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/eur/2014/964/images/eur_20140964_2019-02-16_en_003)

<table>
<tfoot>
<tr><td colspan="2">Commission Regulation (EU) No 1407/2013 of 18 December 2013 on the application of Articles 107 and 108 of the Treaty on the Functioning of the European Union to de minimis aid ( <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><a href="https://webarchive.nationalarchives.gov.uk/eu-exit/https://eur-lex.europa.eu/legal-content/EN/TXT/?uri=uriserv:OJ.L_.2013.352.01.0001.01.ENG">OJ L 352, 24.12.2013, p. 1 <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></a>).</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2">Enterprise with less than 250 employees and having a turnover of less than EUR 50 million or total assets less than EUR 43 million; also not belonging to a group exceeding such thresholds. According to the Commission Recommendation, ‘an enterprise is considered to be any entity engaged in an economic activity, irrespective of its legal form’. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2">The following economic sectors are together referred to as the ‘restricted sectors’: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((a)) illegal economic activities: any production, trade or other activity, which is illegal under the laws or regulations of the home jurisdiction for such production, trade or activity. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((b)) Tobacco and distilled alcoholic beverages. The production of and trade in tobacco and distilled alcoholic beverages and related products. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((c)) Production of and trade in weapons and ammunition: the financing of the production of and trade in weapons and ammunition of any kind. This restriction does not apply to the extent such activities are part of or accessory to explicit European Union policies. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((d)) Casinos. Casinos and equivalent enterprises. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((e)) IT sector restrictions. Research, development or technical applications relating to electronic data programs or solutions, which (i) aim specifically at: (a) supporting any activity included in the Restricted Sectors referred to a to d above; (b) internet gambling and online casinos; or (c) pornography, or which (ii) are intended to enable to illegally (a) enter into electronic data networks; or (b) download electronic data. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((f)) Life science sector restrictions. When providing support to the financing of the research, development or technical applications relating to: (i) human cloning for research or therapeutic purposes; or (ii) Genetically Modified Organisms (GMOs). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
</tfoot>
<tbody>
<tr><th><strong>Structure of the financial instrument <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The Capped Portfolio Guarantee shall provide credit risk coverage on a loan by loan basis, for the creation of a portfolio of new loans to SMEs up to a maximum loss amount (cap). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The Capped Portfolio Guarantee shall be made available by the managing authority, in the framework of the operation which is part of the priority axis defined in the programme <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> co-funded by the European Structural and Investment Funds (ESIF) and defined <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> in the context of the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> assessment required in Article 37 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Aim of the instrument <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The aim of the instrument shall be to: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((1)) Provide better access to finance to targeted SMEs, addressing concrete and well identified market gaps. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((2)) Leverage of the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> ESIFs <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>programmes <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> to support financing for SMEs as referred in Article 37(4) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> ESIF <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> programme contribution from the managing authority takes the form of a guarantee fund managed by a financial intermediary. This contribution shall not crowd out guarantees available from other public or private investors. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The guarantee fund managed by the financial intermediary shall commit to provide funds from the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> ESIF <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> programme to the financial institutions building up portfolios of new loans in case of default of the final recipients. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>In the case of fund of funds structure, the fund of funds shall transfer the contribution from the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> ESIF <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> programme to the financial intermediary. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The Capped Guarantee Instrument shall be implemented to cover a portfolio of new loans build up by one or more financial institutions. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The financial institutions building up portfolios of new loans shall count on a partial guarantee covering losses up to a capped amount when providing loans to eligible SMEs. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The financial advantage of the guarantee must be passed on to the final recipients (e.g. as a reduction of the interest rate of the loans or/and collateral reduction but always with a full financial advantage of the programme public contribution passed on to the final recipients). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>State aid implication <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The Capped Portfolio Guarantee shall be designed as a State aid free instrument, i.e. market conform at the level of the financial intermediary managing the guarantee fund and financial institutions building up portfolios of new loans and aid to the final recipients under the applicable <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>de minimis <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> Regulation. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>(a)<br><em>At the level of the fund of funds, the financial intermediary managing the guarantee fund, the financial institutions building up portfolios of new loans the aid is excluded when: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em><br>(1)<br>The remuneration (i.e. management costs and/or fees) of the financial intermediary and the fund of funds reflects the current market remuneration in comparable situations, which is the case when the latter has been selected in an open, transparent, objective and non-discriminatory selection procedure or if the remuneration is aligned with the Articles 12 and 13 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 480/2014, and no other advantage is granted by the State. Where the fund of fund only transfers the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> ESIF <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>programme <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> contribution to the financial intermediary, and has a public interest mission, and has no commercial activity when implementing the measure, and is not co-investing with its own resources — therefore it is not considered a beneficiary of aid, — it is enough that the fund of fund is not overcompensated and <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>(2)<br>The financial institution shall be selected through an open, transparent, non-discriminatory and objective selection procedure to build up the portfolio of new loans with its own resources and the risk retained by the financial institution is in no case less than 20 % of the loan amount (on a loan by loan basis), and <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>(3)<br>In addition, the financial advantage of the programme public contribution to the instrument shall be fully passed on to the final recipients in the form of an interest rate reduction. When selecting the financial intermediary, the managing authority shall, in line with the Article 7(2) of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 480/2014, asses the pricing policy and the methodology to pass on the financial advantage to the final recipients. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Where the financial intermediary does not pass on all the financial advantage to the final recipients, the uncommitted public contribution shall be transferred back to the managing authority. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The guarantee must be linked to a specific financial transaction, for a fixed maximum amount and limited in time. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>(b)<br><em>At the level of final recipients: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em><br>At the SMEs' level, the guaranteed loan shall comply with the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>de minimis <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> rules. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>For each loan inserted within the guaranteed portfolio, the financial intermediary shall calculate the GGE by using the following calculation methodology: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br><strong>Calculation of the GGE = Nominal amount of the loan (EUR) × Cost of risk (standard practice) × Guarantee rate × Guarantee cap rate × Weighted average life of the loan (Years). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong><br>The total amount of aid calculated with the GGE cannot be above EUR 200 000 over a 3 years fiscal period taking into account the cumulation rule for final recipients in the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>de minimis <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> Regulation. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>When the GGE is calculated with the above mentioned formula, for the purpose of a Capped Portfolio Guarantee instrument, the requirement as foreseen in Article 4 of the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>de minimis <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> Regulation <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> is considered to be met. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>A verification mechanism shall ensure that the GGE calculated with the above mentioned formula is not below than the GGE calculated following the Article 4(6)(c) of the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>de minimis <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> Regulation. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Technical support grant or another grant provided to the final recipient shall be cumulated with the calculated GGE. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Regarding SMEs in the fisheries and aquaculture sector, the aid shall comply with the relevant rules of the fisheries de minimis Regulation <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>For activities <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> supported by the EAFRD <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>funded by support for rural development <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>, general rules apply. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Guarantee policy <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>(a)<br><em>Transfer from the managing authority to the financial intermediary: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em><br>Following the signature of a funding agreement between the managing authority and the fund of funds or the financial intermediary, the relevant managing authority transfers contributions from the programme to the fund of funds or to the financial intermediary which places such contributions in a dedicated guarantee fund. The transfer shall be in tranches and respect the ceilings of Article 41 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>(b)<br><em>Origination of a portfolio of new loans: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em><br>The financial institutions shall be required to build up within a pre-determined limited period of time portfolios of new SME loans. Newly originated SME loans are partly covered by the programme contribution on a loan by loan basis up to a certain amount (Cap). Eligible SME loans are automatically included in the portfolio subject to a pre-set loan inclusion criteria. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Inclusion of SME loans shall occur automatically upon receipt by the financial intermediary managing the guarantee fund of an inclusion notice submitted at least on a quarterly basis until the end of the relevant inclusion period. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The financial institutions shall implement a consistent loan policy regarding portfolio diversification, enabling a sound portfolio management and risk diversification, while complying with the applicable industry standards and while remaining appropriate to the managing authority's financial interests and policy objectives. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The identification, selection, due diligence, documentation and execution of the loans for final recipients shall be performed by the financial institutions in accordance with their standard procedures and in accordance with the principles set out in the agreement between the financial intermediary and the financial institution building up a portfolio of new loans. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>(c)<br><em>Loss cover: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em><br>The Capped Portfolio Guarantee shall cover losses incurred by the financial institutions in respect of each defaulted eligible SME loan in accordance with the guarantee rate of a maximum percentage of 80 %. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Losses covered by the Capped Portfolio Guarantee in respect of the portfolio of eligible SME loans shall in aggregate not exceed the cap amount. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The cap amount which is the maximum liability under this instrument is the product of the volume of the target loan portfolio multiplied by the guarantee rate and the guarantee cap rate. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The guarantee cap rate shall be determined as part of the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> risk assessment in accordance with Article 42(1)(b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 and Article 8 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 480/2014. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Losses covered are principal amounts due, payable and outstanding and standard interest (but excluding late payment, fees and any other costs and expenses). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>(d)<br><em>Guarantee payment: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em><br>Following the occurrence of a loss related to a default, the financial intermediary managing the guarantee fund shall make guarantee payments to the financial institution under the Guarantee within typically 60 days. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Pricing and collateral policies <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The financial intermediary shall present a methodology that ensures the full pass on of the financial advantage of the programme public contribution to the eligible SMEs. The financial institution shall have a pricing/collateral policy in line with the methodology. The pricing/collateral policy and the methodology shall include the following elements: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((1)) The instrument shall cover a maximum of 80 % of the risk exposure of each eligible SME Loan (up to a Cap). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((2)) The entire financial advantage of the programme public contribution shall be passed on to the eligible SMEs, through a reduction of the interest rate charged and/or a reduction of the collateral required by the financial institution. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((3)) The GGE calculation as presented in the State aid section shall be applied for each loan included in the portfolio. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((4)) No Guarantee fees shall be charged to the financial institution by the financial intermediary managing the guarantee fund. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((5)) The financial institution shall reduce the overall interest rate and/or collateral requirement under each eligible SME loan included in the portfolio following the pricing policy and methodology ensuring the full pass on of the financial advantage. The level of such reduction proposed by the financial institution shall be assessed and confirmed by the financial intermediary following the relevant analysis and due diligence and shall deemed to be an eligibility criterion for SME loans to be included in the Portfolio. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((6)) The managing authority may decide based on the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> assessment which identifies the targeted SMEs and the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> risk assessment that determines the risk, to require guarantee fees payable by the final recipients. In such case, the GGE shall be calculated with the formula presented in the section on State aid here above or be aligned with the conditions of the Guarantee Notice. The fees paid by the final recipients shall be paid back to the guarantee fund as resources returned in the sense of Article 43 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((7)) The pricing policy and the methodology shall remain constant during the eligibility period. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Guarantee to financial institution amount and rate (product details) <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The Capped Portfolio Guarantee shall respect the conditions set in Article 8 of the Delegated Regulation (EU) No 480/2014. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The Guarantee Cap rate shall be determined in the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> risk assessment in accordance with Article 42(1)(b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 and Article 8 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 480/2014 and in all cases not exceed 25 %. The guarantee may cover expected and unexpected losses. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The multiplier of the guarantee financed by the programme contribution is defined as: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Multiplier = (1/Guarantee Rate) × (1/Guarantee Cap Rate). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The multiplier ratio shall be based on the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> risk assessment and be equal to or higher than 5. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The size of the target portfolio partially covered by the guarantee shall be based on the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> assessment findings justifying the support to the financial instrument (Article 37 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013) and take into account the revolving approach of the instrument (if applicable). The composition of the targeted portfolio of loans shall be defined in a way to ensure diversification of risk. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Guarantee to financial institution (activities) <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The loan portfolio guaranteed by the guarantee instrument shall include newly originated loans provided to the final recipients, to the exclusion of refinancing of existing loans. The eligibility criteria for inclusion in the portfolio are determined pursuant to the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Union <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>national <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> law (e.g. Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 and Fund-specific rules), programme, national eligibility rules, and with the financial intermediary with the aim of reaching a large number of final recipients and achieving sufficient portfolio diversification. Financial institutions shall have a reasonable estimation of the portfolio risk profile (concentration limit by sector for example). These criteria shall reflect market conditions and practices <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> in the relevant country or region <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>.<br>The financial institution shall estimate <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> a recovery rate to be used for calculating the amount expected to be recovered from the defaults in the portfolio, which impacts the evaluation of the guarantee cap rate. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Managing authority's liability <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The managing authority's liability in relation to the financial instrument shall be as set out in Article 6 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 480/2014. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Default means, in respect of a loan to final recipient that (i) the financial institution can prove at any time (acting in accordance with its internal procedures and as reflected in its financial and regulatory reporting) that a final recipient is unlikely to meet its payment obligations; or (ii) a final recipient has failed to meet any payment obligation under the relevant SME loan which has continued for at least 90 consecutive calendar days <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Duration <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The guarantee period of the financial instrument shall be set in order to ensure that the programme contribution as referred in Article 42 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 is used with guarantees of loans disbursed to final recipients no later than the 31 December 2023. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The typical duration to create the portfolio of guaranteed loans is recommended to be up to 4 years from the date of signature of the funding agreement (between the managing authority or fund of funds and the financial intermediary). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Risk-sharing at FI level (alignment of interest) <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>Alignment of interest between the managing authority, the financial intermediary and the financial institution shall be achieved through: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The own credit risk retained by the financial institution in no case shall be less than 20 % on a loan by loan basis. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The financial institution commits to build up a portfolio of new loans with its own resources. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The financial advantage of the Capped Guarantee is fully passed on to final recipient SMEs. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Performance fees for financial intermediary as provided by Articles 12 and 13 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 480/2014. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Eligible Financial Intermediaries and Financial Institutions <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>Financial intermediaries shall be public and private bodies established in a <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Member State <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>constituent nation <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> which are legally authorised to provide guarantees on loans to enterprises operating in the jurisdiction of the programme which contributes to the financial instrument. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Financial institutions shall be public and private bodies established in a <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Member States <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>constituent nation <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> which are legally authorised to provide loans to enterprises operating in the jurisdiction of the programme which contributes to the financial instrument. Such bodies are financial institutions, and, as appropriate, microfinance institutions or any other institution authorised to provide loans. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Final Recipient (final recipients) eligibility <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The final recipients shall be eligible under <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Union and <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> national law, the relevant programme, and funding agreement. The final recipients shall fulfil the following eligibility criteria at the date of the document evidencing the relevant SME guarantee, meaning the guarantee commitment: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((a)) shall be a micro, small and medium enterprise (SMEs (including individual entrepreneurs/self-employed persons) as defined in the Commission Recommendation 2003/361/EC <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>.<br>((b)) shall not be a SME active in the sectors defined in points (d)-(f) of Article 1 of the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>de minimis <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> Regulation. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((c)) shall not be part of one or more restricted sectors <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>.<br>((d)) shall not be a firm in difficulty as defined by State aid rule. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((e)) shall not be delinquent or in default in respect of any other loan or lease either granted by the financial intermediary or by another financial institution pursuant to checks made in accordance with the financial intermediary internal guidelines and standard credit policy. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>In addition, at the time of the investment and during the reimbursement of the guaranteed loan, final recipients shall have a registered place of business in a <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Member State <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>constituent nation <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> and the economic activity for which the guaranteed loan was disbursed shall be located in the relevant <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Member State and Region/Jurisdiction <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>constituent nation <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>of the ESIF <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> programme. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Characteristics of the product for the final recipients <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The financial institution shall deliver to final recipients the loans that contribute to the objective of the programme and that are guaranteed by the programme under the Capped guarantee portfolio. The terms of the guarantees and of the loans shall be grounded on the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> assessment referred to in Article 37(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The loans shall be used exclusively for the following permitted purposes: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((a)) Investments in tangible and intangible assets including transfer of proprietary rights in enterprises provided that such transfer takes place between independent investors. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((b)) Working capital related to development or expansion activities that are ancillary (and linked) to activities referred to in (a) above (which ancillary nature shall be evidenced, inter alia, by the business plan of the final recipient and the amount of the financing). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The following eligibility criteria shall be met at all times by the loans included in the portfolio: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((c)) Loans shall be newly originated, to the exclusion of refinancing existing loans. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((d)) The guaranteed part of the underlying loan included in the portfolio i) shall be up to EUR 1 500 000 based on the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> assessment and ii) shall be provided under such conditions that would not cause the GGE with respect to each final recipient to exceed EUR 200 000 (or EUR 100 000 in the road freight transport and EUR 30 000 in the fishery and aquaculture sectors) over any period of three fiscal years. Eligible SMEs could potentially apply more than once for loans allocated in the context of this financial instrument provided that the above-mentioned GGE limit is fully respected. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((e)) Loans shall provide financing for one or more of the permitted purposes in EUR and/or national currency in the relevant jurisdiction and, as the case may be, in any other currency. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((f)) Loans shall not be in the form of mezzanine loans, subordinated debt or quasi equity. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((g)) Loans shall not be in the form of revolving credit lines. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((h)) Shall have a repayment schedule, including regular amortising and/or bullet payments. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((i)) Loans shall not finance pure financial activities or real estate development when undertaken as a financial investment activity and shall not finance the provision of consumer finance. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((j)) Shall have between a minimum maturity of 12 months and a maximum maturity of 120 months. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Reporting and targeted results. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>Financial intermediaries shall provide the managing authority or fund of funds with at least quarterly information in a standardised form and scope. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The report shall include all the relevant elements for the managing authority to fulfil the provisions of Article 46 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Member States <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>The relevant authority <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> shall also fulfil their reporting obligations pursuant to the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>de minimis <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> Regulation. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Indicators must be aligned with the specific objectives of the relevant priority of the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> ESIF <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> programme financing the financial instrument and on the expected results of the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> assessment. They shall be measured and reported at least quarterly for the guarantee fundand aligned as a minimum with the regulation requirements. In addition to the common indicators of the priority axis of the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> ESIF <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> programme (employment increase, number of SMEs,…) other indicators are: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Numbers of loans guaranteed <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Volume of the loans guaranteed <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Number of loans defaulted <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Value of the loans defaulted <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Guarantees committed/called (number, amounts) <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Resources uncalled and gains (e.g. interest generated) <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Evaluation of the economic benefit of the programme contribution <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The financial advantage of the programme public contribution to the instrument shall be fully passed on to the final recipients (benefit of the guarantee). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The financial advantage for the eligible SMEs shall be evidenced by a reduction of the overall interest rate required by the financial institution and/or collateral reduction on such SME loan. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The financial intermediary shall monitor and report on the GGE for final recipients as referred in the State aid section. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>These principles shall be reflected in the agreements between the managing authority or fund of funds and the financial intermediaries and between the financial intermediaries and the financial institutions building up portfolios of new loans. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

52][^f00016][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^f00017][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-bcfc252bce0cba0cb0ccf1f48174d7ba][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-4c4b4cb3e2d8aec6470964bbe7317c7b][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-8ddcb717539d6e08dcbc8904327e989c][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-6c3566e7e0dd7effda4d61724653378f][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-82488de9bc5df092d7692f50e07912e8][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-82488de9bc5df092d7692f50e07912e8][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852]

## ANNEX IV (affected by repeal, see note) — Loan for energy efficiency and renewable energies in the residential building sector (Renovation loan) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852]

**Schematic representation of the Renovation loan principle *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852]**

[image: see source](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/eur/2014/964/images/eur_20140964_2019-02-16_en_004)

<table>
<tfoot>
<tr><td colspan="2">Commission Regulation (EU) No 1407/2013 of 18 December 2013 on the application of Articles 107 and 108 of the Treaty on the Functioning of the European Union to de minimis aid ( <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><a href="https://webarchive.nationalarchives.gov.uk/eu-exit/https://eur-lex.europa.eu/legal-content/EN/TXT/?uri=uriserv:OJ.L_.2013.352.01.0001.01.ENG">OJ L 352, 24.12.2013, p. 1 <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></a>).</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2">The following economic sectors are together referred to as the ‘restricted sectors’. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((a)) illegal economic activities: any production, trade or other activity, which is illegal under the laws or regulations of the home jurisdiction for such production, trade or activity. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((b)) Tobacco and distilled alcoholic beverages. The production of and trade in tobacco and distilled alcoholic beverages and related products. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((c)) Production of and trade in weapons and ammunition: the financing of the production of and trade in weapons and ammunition of any kind. This restriction does not apply to the extent such activities are part of or accessory to explicit European Union policies. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((d)) Casinos. Casinos and equivalent enterprises. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((e)) IT sector restrictions. Research, development or technical applications relating to electronic data programs or solutions, which (i) aim specifically at: (a) supporting any activity included in the Restricted Sectors referred to a to d above; (b) internet gambling and online casinos; or (c) pornography, or which (ii) are intended to enable to illegally (a) enter into electronic data networks; or (b) download electronic data. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((f)) Life science sector restrictions. When providing support to the financing of the research, development or technical applications relating to: (i) human cloning for research or therapeutic purposes; or (ii) Genetically Modified Organisms (GMOs). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2">As defined in Commission Decision 2012/21/EU of 20 December 2011 as disadvantaged citizens or socially less advantaged groups who due to solvency constraints are unable to obtain housing at market conditions. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
</tfoot>
<tbody>
<tr><th><strong>Structure of the financial instrument <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The Renovation loan shall take the form of a loan fund to be set up by a financial intermediary with contributions from the programme and the financial intermediary itself to finance a portfolio of newly originated loans, to the exclusion of the refinancing of existing loans. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The Renovation loan shall be made available, in the framework of the operation which is part of the priority axis defined in the programme <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> funded by the ESIF <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> and defined in the context of the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> assessment required in Article 37 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Aim of the instrument <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The aim of the instrument is to offer preferential loans to natural and legal persons or independent professionals owning premises (apartment, social housing or individual household), as well as administrators or other legal bodies acting on behalf and for the benefit of the owners in order to undertake renovation works that are eligible for <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> ESIF support <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>support for rural development and support under Regulation 508/2014 <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>.<br>The <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> ESIF <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> programme contribution from the managing authority to a financial intermediary shall not crowd out financing available from other private or public investors. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> ESIF <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> programme shall provide funding to the financial intermediary in order to build up a portfolio of newly generated loans, and in parallel, participate in the losses/defaults and recoveries on the loans in this portfolio on a loan by loan basis and in the same proportion as the programme contribution in the instrument. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>In the case of fund of funds structure, the fund of funds shall transfer the contribution from the ESIF programme to the financial intermediary. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>In addition to the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> ESIF <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> programme contribution, the fund of funds may provide its own resources which are combined with the financial intermediary resources. The fund of funds shall in this case take the pro-rata part of the risk sharing between the different contributions in the portfolio of loans. State aid rules have to be respected also in relation to such resources if they are public resources. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>State aid implication <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The Renovation Loan shall be designed as a State aid free instrument, i.e. market-conform remuneration for the financial intermediary, full pass-on of financial advantage by the financial intermediary to the final recipients, and the financing provided to the final recipients are under the applicable <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>de minimis <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> Regulation. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>(a)<br><em>Aid at the level of the financial intermediary and the fund of funds is excluded when: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em><br>(1)<br>the financial intermediary and the managing authority or fund of funds bear at any time the losses and benefits in proportion to their contribution (pro-rata) and there is an economically significant participation of the financial intermediary in the Renovation loan instrument, and <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>(2)<br>The remuneration (i.e. management costs and/or fees) of the financial intermediary and the fund of funds reflects the current market remuneration in comparable situations, which is the case when the they have been selected through an open, transparent, non-discriminatory and objective selection procedure or if their remuneration is aligned with the Articles 12 and 13 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 480/2014 and no other advantages are granted by the State. Where the fund of fund only transfers the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> ESIF <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>programme <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> contribution to the financial intermediary, and has a public interest mission, and has no commercial activity when implementing the measure, and is not co-investing with its own resources — therefore it is not considered a beneficiary of aid, — it is enough that the fund of fund is not overcompensated, and <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>(3)<br>The financial advantage of the programme public contribution to the instrument shall be fully passed on to the final recipients in the form of an interest rate reduction. When selecting the financial intermediary, the managing authority shall, in line with the Article 7(2) of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 480/2014, asses the pricing policy and the methodology to pass on the financial advantage to the final recipients. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Where the financial intermediary does not pass on all the financial advantage to the final recipients, the undisbursed public contribution shall be transferred back to the managing authority. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>(b)<br><em>Aid at the level of an entity acting on behalf of the owners (i.e. natural and legal persons, independent professionals owning premises, administrators, other legal bodies): <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em><br>Aid at the level of an entity acting on behalf of the owners is excluded when: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((1)) the entity does not benefit from any direct transfers of public support and <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((2)) The entity transfers all the financial advantages of the programme public contribution to the final recipients <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>(c)<br><em>At the level of the owners without or with an economic activity (legal person or independent professionals, landlords and owners who install renewable energies, supplying part of the energy produced to the grid): <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em><br>Owners that are natural persons and which are not considered undertakings because they do not exercise an economic activity are not considered to be beneficiaries of State aid. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Owners with an economic activity qualify as an ‘undertaking’ and are subject to State aid rules. In particular, this is the case if they are landlords (renting is an economic activity) and in the case of installing renewable energies, if part of the renewable energies produced is supplied to the grid (supplying energy to the grid is considered an economic activity). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>At the level of the owners with an economic activity, the aid shall comply with the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>de minimis <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> rules. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>For each loan inserted within the portfolio regarding owners with an economic activity, the financial intermediary shall calculate the GGE by using the following calculation methodology: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br><strong>Calculation of the GGE = Nominal amount of the loan (EUR) × (Cost of funding (standard practice) + Cost of risk (standard practice) – Any fees charged by the managing authority on the programme contribution to the financial intermediary) × Weighted average life of the loan (Years) × Risk sharing rate. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong><br>When the GGE is calculated with the above mentioned formula, for the purpose of the Renovation loan instrument, the requirement as foreseen in Article 4 of the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>de minimis <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> Regulation <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> is considered to be met. There is no minimum collateral requirement. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>A verification mechanism shall ensure that the GGE calculated with the above mentioned formula is not below than the GGE calculated following the Article 4(3)(c) of the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>de minimis <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> Regulation. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The total amount of aid calculated with the GGE cannot be above EUR 200 000 over a 3 years fiscal period taking into account the cumulation rule for final recipients in the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>de minimis <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> Regulation. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Technical support grant or another grant provided to the final recipient shall be cumulated with the calculated GGE. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Lending policy <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>(a)<br><em>Disbursement from the managing authority or fund of funds to the financial intermediary: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em><br>Following the signature of a funding agreement between the managing authority and the fund of funds or the financial intermediary, the relevant managing authority transfers public contributions from the programme to the fund of funds or the financial intermediary which places such contributions in a dedicated Renovation loan fund. The transfer shall be in tranches and respect the ceilings of Article 41 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The target lending volume and range of interest rate shall be confirmed within the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> assessment in accordance with Article 37 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 and shall be taken into account to determine the nature of the instrument (revolving or non-revolving instrument). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The maximum risk-sharing of the financial instrument towards the final recipients shall be 85 % (i.e. at least 15 % shall be provided by the financial intermediary own funds). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>(b)<br><em>Origination of a portfolio of new loans: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em><br>The financial intermediary shall be required to originate within a pre-determined limited period of time a portfolio of new loans funded according to a risk-sharing rate agreed in the funding agreement (i.e. funded by (i) the programme contribution) (ii) the financial intermediary own fund). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Eligible loans pre-defined according to eligibility criteria on a loan-by-loan basis and portfolio level shall be automatically included in the portfolio by way of submitting inclusion notices at least on a quarterly basis. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The financial intermediary shall implement a consistent lending policy, especially regarding portfolio composition enabling a sound credit portfolio management and risk diversification, while aiming at reducing the market failure identified in the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> assessment (referring to Article 37 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013) and while remaining aligned with the managing authority's financial interests and policy objectives. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The identification, selection, due diligence, documentation and execution of the loans to final recipients shall be performed by the financial intermediary in accordance with its standard procedures and in accordance with the principles set out in the relevant funding agreement. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>(c)<br><em>Re-use of resources paid back to financial instrument: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em><br>Resources paid back to the financial instrument shall be either reused within the same financial instrument (revolving within the same financial instrument) or after being paid back to the managing authority or the fund of funds they shall be used in accordance with Article 44 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>When revolving within the same financial instrument, as a matter of principles, the amounts that are attributable to <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> the support of the ESIF <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>support for rural development and support under Regulation 508/2014 <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> and that are reimbursed and/or recovered by the financial intermediary from loans to final recipients within the time framework for investments shall be made available for new use within the same financial instrument. This revolving approach as referred at Articles 44 and 45 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 shall be included in the funding agreement. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Alternatively, if managing authority or fund of funds is directly repaid, the repayments shall occur regularly mirroring (i) principal repayments (on a pro rata basis on the basis of the risk sharing rate) (ii) any recovered amounts and losses deductions (according to the risk sharing rate), of the Renovation loans and (iii) any interest rate payments. These resources have to be used in accordance with Articles 44 and 45 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>(d)<br><em>Loss recoveries: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em><br>The financial intermediary shall take recovery actions in relation to each defaulted loan co-financed by the Renovation loan in accordance with its internal guidelines and procedures. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Amounts recovered by the financial intermediary (net of recovery and foreclosure costs, if any) shall be allocated pro-rata to the risk-sharing between the financial intermediary and the managing authority or the fund of funds. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>(e)<br><em>Others: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em><br>Interest and other gains generated by support <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> from the ESI Funds <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>for rural development and support under Regulation 508/2014 <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> to financial instrument shall be used in accordance with Article 43 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Pricing policy <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>When proposing its pricing, the financial intermediary shall present a pricing policy and the methodology to ensure the full pass on of the financial advantage of the programme public contribution to the final recipients. The pricing policy and the methodology shall include the following elements: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((1)) the interest rate on the financial intermediary participation is set at market basis (i.e. according to the financial intermediary own policy), <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((2)) the overall interest rate, to be charged on loans to the final recipients included in the portfolio, must be reduced proportionally to the allocation provided by the public contribution of the programme. This reduction shall take into account the fees that the managing authority might charge on the programme contribution. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((3)) The GGE calculation as presented in the State aid section shall be applied on each loan included in the Portfolio. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((4)) The pricing policy and the methodology shall remain constant during the eligibility period. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Programme contribution to financial instrument: amount and rate (product details) <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The Renovation loan allocation to financial intermediaries and the minimum risk sharing rate shall be based on the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> assessment findings justifying the support to the financial instrument (Article 37 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013) and taking into account the revolving approach of the instrument (if applicable). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Programme contribution to financial instrument (activities) <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The loan portfolio funded by the Renovation loan instrument shall include newly originated loans to the final recipients, to the exclusion of the refinancing of existing loans. The eligibility criteria for inclusion in the portfolio are determined pursuant to <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> the Union <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>national <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> law (e.g. Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 and Fund-specific rules), programme, national eligibility rules, and with the financial intermediary with the aim of reaching a large number of final recipients and achieving sufficient portfolio diversification and homogeneity in order to allow a reasonable estimation of the portfolio risk profile. These criteria shall reflect market conditions and practices in the relevant country or region. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The financial intermediary shall be required to cooperate with regional or national bodies responsible for providing additional services in relation to the implementation of the renovation projects, which includes inter alia: consultancy services; verification and evaluation of project preparation, construction, technical supervision and procurement documents; evaluation of the compliance of renovation projects with <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Union and <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> national law; the provision of grant support, State aid verification and registration. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Managing authority's liability <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The managing authority's liability in relation to the financial instrument shall be as set out in Article 6 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 480/2014. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Duration <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The lending period of the financial instrument shall be set in order to ensure that the programme contribution as referred in Article 42 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 is used for loans disbursed to final recipients no later than by 31 December 2023. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Lending and risk-sharing at financial intermediary level (alignment of interest) <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>Alignment of interest between the managing authority and the financial intermediary shall be achieved through: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Performance fees as provided by Articles 12 and 13 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 480/2014. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The financial intermediary must contribute under local market conditions to the financing with at least 15 % of the total financing commitment for lending to final recipients (allowing determining the risk sharing rate). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The losses and recoveries shall impact pro-rata the financial intermediary and the managing authority within their respective liability. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Eligible financial intermediaries <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>Public and private bodies established in a <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Member State <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> constituent nation <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> which shall be legally authorised to provide Renovation loans to persons owning premises and enterprises operating and owning premises in the jurisdiction of the programme which contributes to the financial instrument. Such bodies are financial institutions, and as appropriate microfinance institutions or any other institution authorised to provide loans. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Final recipient eligibility <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The final recipients shall be eligible under <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Union and <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> national law, the relevant priority, and funding agreement. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Final recipients shall be natural or legal persons or independent professionals (economic activity), as well as administrators or other legal bodies acting on behalf and for the benefit of owners, owning premises (apartment or individual household) implementing energy efficiency or renewable energies measures that are eligible under Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 and programme support. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Within the eligibility rules under the programme and in line with national <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> and Union <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> rules the following examples of types of works may be eligible: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Technical support for the preparation of the part of the project relating to the energy efficiency or renewable energies measures. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Implementation costs of the part of the project relating to the energy efficiency or renewable energies measures. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Major repairs or replacement of heating and hot water systems: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Replacement or refitting of the heating substation or the boiler house (individual boilers) as well as hot water preparation systems. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Installation of balancing valves for stands. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Improvement of heat insulation for pipes. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Replacement of pipes and heating devices. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Installation of individual heating measurement system and thermostatic valves in apartments. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Replacement or refitting of hot water system pipes and installations. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Replacement or refitting of ventilation system. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Replacement of windows and entrance doors. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Roof insulation, including construction of a new sloping roof (excluding construction of premises in the attic). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Insulation of façade walls. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Insulation of cellar ceiling. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Installation of alternative energy sources (sun, wind, etc.) systems. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Major repairs or replacement of elevators by replacing them by more energy efficient elevators. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Replacement or repair of the common use engineering systems of the building (sewage system, electric installations, fire prevention installations, drinking water pipelines and installations ventilation system). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Regarding the final recipients, the following eligibility criteria shall apply when lending to final recipient/owners with an economic activity under a legal entity (for example independent professionals). The eligibility criteria shall be met at the date of the signature of the loan: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((a)) shall be a micro, small and medium enterprise (SMEs (including individual entrepreneurs/self-employed persons) as defined in the Commission Recommendation 2003/361/EC. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((b)) shall not be a SME active in the sectors defined in points (a) — (f) of Article 1 of the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>de minimis <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> Regulation. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((c)) shall not be part of on one or more restricted sectors <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>.<br>((d)) shall not be a firm in difficulty as defined by State aid rules. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>((e)) shall not be delinquent or in default in respect of any other loan or lease either granted by the financial intermediary or by another financial institution pursuant to checks made in accordance with the financial intermediary internal guidelines and standard credit policy. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>In addition, at the time of the investment and during the reimbursement of the loan, final recipients shall have a registered place of business in a <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Member State <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>constituent nation <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> and the economic activity for which the loan was disbursed shall be located in the relevant <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Member State and Region/Jurisdiction <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>constituent nation <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>of the ESIF programme <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>.</td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Characteristics of the product for the final recipients <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The financial intermediary shall deliver to final recipients new loans that contribute to the objective of the programme and that are co-financed by the programme under the Renovation loan, to the exclusion of the refinancing of existing loans. Their terms shall be grounded on the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> assessment referred to in Article 37 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The Renovation loan maturity shall be for a period of up to 20 years. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The maximum amount of each Renovation loan shall be fixed in relation with the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> assessment findings justifying the programme contribution to the financial instrument and shall be fixed in the funding agreement between the managing authority, fund of funds and the financial intermediary. The maximum amount per loan per individual household shall not exceed EUR 75 000. Loans for a building administrator are the sum of individual households of the building. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The financial instrument may require from the final recipients or the administrators of common property acting on behalf of final recipients, an ‘own funds’ contribution. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The Renovation loan shall be subject to <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> annual fixed interest rate <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> and shall include regular amortisation. The interest rate on the financial intermediary participation is set at market basis. The interest rate applicable to the relevant eligible loan included in the portfolio shall be reduced by the proportion of the programme public contribution in favour of the final recipients. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>An interest rate subsidy, as under Article 37(7) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, may be awarded to low income households or vulnerable households <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> The maximum interest rate subsidy shall correspond to the interest rate to be paid by the low income households or vulnerable households on the contribution of the financial intermediary in each loan. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Certain technical support costs may be included in the financial instrument <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> in the context of Article 37(7) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. Support must be provided for project preparation only (preparatory studies and assisting in the preparation of the investment up to the investment decision). These technical support costs shall only be eligible in the event that a Renovation loan is signed between the financial intermediary and the final recipients and regardless of the entity that provides these services (e.g. regardless of whether the financial intermediary provides such services or they are obtained from another entity). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Reporting and targeted results <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>Financial intermediaries shall provide the managing authority or fund of funds with at least quarterly information in a standardised form and scope. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The report shall include all the relevant elements for the managing authority to fulfil the conditions of Article 46 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Member States <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>The relevant authority <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> shall also fulfil their reporting obligations pursuant to the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>de minimis <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> Regulation. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Indicators must be aligned with the specific objectives of the relevant priority of the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> ESIF <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> programme financing the financial instrument and on the expected results of the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>ex-ante <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></em> assessment. They shall be measured and reported at least quarterly for the Renovation loan and aligned as a minimum with the regulation requirements. In addition to the common indicators of the priority axis of the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> ESIF <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> programme (number of households with improved energy consumption classification, estimated annual decrease of GHG …) other indicators are: <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Number and volume of the loans <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Renovated family houses (square meters) <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Renovated apartments in buildings (square meters). <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Defaults (numbers and amounts) <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Resources repaid and gains <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Number and amounts of the technical support <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Number and amounts of interest rate subsidies <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>Evaluation of the economic benefit of the programme contribution <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></strong></th><td>The financial intermediary shall reduce the overall effective interest rate (and collateral policy where appropriate) charged to the final recipients under each eligible loan included in the portfolio reflecting the favourable funding and risk sharing conditions of the Renovation loan. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>The entire financial advantage of the programme public contribution to the instrument shall be transferred to the final recipients in the form of an interest rate reduction. The financial intermediary shall monitor and report on the GGE for final recipients as referred in the State aid section. This principle shall be reflected in the funding agreement between the managing authority or fund of funds and the financial intermediary. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## ANNEX V (affected by repeal, see note) — CO-INVESTMENT FACILITY (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852][^c-26aecc2305386e614658383de410c852]
